AR Lab Generation — Benchmark Success/Failure Summary¶

LLM-generated ARLEM lab specifications, benchmarked across providers, models, prompt-specificity levels (L1/L3/L4), output specs (json_lab, arlem, arlem_simple), and lab topics.

This is the success/failure companion to the statistics report (benchmark_statistics, which covers what the successful runs produced). Here we look at which cells generated at all and why the failures failed. Cost/token figures are summed from each cell's tracking record. The failure taxonomy comes from benchmark_dataframe.fail_mode (schema validation = the model's output failed Pydantic/ARLEM cross-validation — real model signal; other = everything else, including the upstream Gemini multi-function-call retry-death, issue #53).

The tables count both successes and failures; every 'X of Y' is successes over attempts for that slice.

In [1]:

df = pd.DataFrame(RECORDS)

# Order axes by what's actually present, not a fixed assumption.
LEVELS = [lv for lv in ['L1', 'L2', 'L3', 'L4'] if lv in set(df.level)]
PROVIDERS = [p for p in ['openai', 'anthropic', 'google'] if p in set(df.provider)]
SPECS = [s for s in ['json_lab', 'arlem', 'arlem_simple'] if s in set(df.spec_type)]

def _frac(d):
    return f"{int(d.success.sum())}/{len(d)}" if len(d) else '-'

def success_by_provider_level(sub):
    rows = []
    for p in PROVIDERS:
        d = sub[sub.provider == p]
        row = {'provider': p}
        for lv in LEVELS:
            row[lv] = _frac(d[d.level == lv])
        row['total'] = _frac(d)
        rows.append(row)
    return pd.DataFrame(rows).set_index('provider')

def success_by_spec_level(sub):
    rows = []
    for s in SPECS:
        d = sub[sub.spec_type == s]
        row = {'spec': s}
        for lv in LEVELS:
            row[lv] = _frac(d[d.level == lv])
        row['total'] = _frac(d)
        rows.append(row)
    return pd.DataFrame(rows).set_index('spec')

def model_grid(sub):
    # One row per model; per-level cell is successes/attempts across all
    # specs and labs in `sub` (L3/L4 fan out to 3 specs, so not 0/1).
    rows = []
    order = sub.sort_values(['provider', 'model']).display_name.unique()
    for m in order:
        d = sub[sub.display_name == m]
        row = {'model': m}
        for lv in LEVELS:
            row[lv] = _frac(d[d.level == lv])
        row['ok'] = _frac(d)
        fm = [x for x in d[~d.success].fail_mode.tolist() if x]
        row['top fail mode'] = max(set(fm), key=fm.count) if fm else ''
        rows.append(row)
    return pd.DataFrame(rows).set_index('model')

def slice_stats(sub):
    ok = int(sub.success.sum()); n = len(sub)
    succ = sub[sub.success & (sub.level != 'L1')]
    return {
        'success': f"{ok}/{n} ({ok/n:.0%})" if n else '-',
        'wall_min': round(float(sub.wall_s.sum()) / 60, 1),
        'cost_usd': round(float(sub.cost_usd.sum()), 2),
        'avg_objects_L3-4': round(float(succ.num_objects.mean()), 1) if len(succ) else 0,
        'avg_clips_L3-4': round(float(succ.num_clips.mean()), 1) if len(succ) else 0,
    }

def fail_table(sub):
    fm = sub[~sub.success].fail_mode
    if not len(fm):
        return None
    return fm.value_counts().rename_axis('mode').to_frame('failed runs')

ft = fail_table(df)
if ft is not None:
    print('Failure modes (whole window):')
    display(ft)
else:
    print('No failures in window.')

In [3]:
import numpy as np
rate = lambda d, p: (d[d.provider == p].success.mean() * 100) if len(d[d.provider == p]) else 0
x = np.arange(len(PROVIDERS)); w = 0.6
fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(8, 4.2))
bars = ax.bar(x, [rate(df, p) for p in PROVIDERS], w, color='#4a7')
ax.set_xticks(x); ax.set_xticklabels([p.capitalize() for p in PROVIDERS])
ax.set_ylabel('Success rate (%)'); ax.set_ylim(0, 105)
ax.set_title('Generation success by provider (whole window)')
for b in bars:
    ax.annotate(f'{b.get_height():.0f}%', (b.get_x()+b.get_width()/2, b.get_height()+1), ha='center', fontsize=9)
plt.tight_layout(); plt.show()

Per-lab breakdown¶

Each lab topic swept across every model × level × spec. The per-model grid shows successes/attempts at each level (L3/L4 fan out to three specs, so a cell of 2/3 means one spec failed).

for lab in sorted(df.lab_name.dropna().unique()):

    ft = fail_table(sub)
    if ft is not None:
        print('Failure modes:')
        display(ft)
    else:
        print('No failures.')
    print()

n = len(df); ok = int(df.success.sum())
print(f'Overall: {ok}/{n} cells generated ({ok/n:.1%}).')
fails = df[~df.success]
if len(fails):
    print(f'{len(fails)} failures, by mode:')
    for mode, c in fails.fail_mode.value_counts().items():
        print(f'  - {mode or "(unclassified)"}: {c}')
    print('Failures by provider:',
          fails.provider.value_counts().to_dict())
    print('Failures by spec:',
          fails.spec_type.value_counts().to_dict())
    worst = (df.groupby('display_name').success.mean().sort_values().head(3))
    print('Lowest success-rate models:',
          {k: f'{v:.0%}' for k, v in worst.items()})
else:
    print('No failures in the window.')
